#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

// FNV-1a hash, 64 bit length
class hash {
  public:
	using digest = uint64_t;

	template <typename T>
	void add(const T& value) {
		const unsigned char* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const unsigned char*>(&value);
		for(size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(T); ++i) {
			d = (d ^ bytes[i]) * 0x100000001b3ull;
		}
	}

	digest get() const { return d; }

  private:
	digest d = 0xcbf29ce484222325ull;
};
#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

cmake_minimum_required(VERSION 3.10)
project(cahn_hilliard_benchmark)

set(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD 20)
set(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD_REQUIRED ON)

add_executable(cahn_hilliard cahn_hilliard.cpp)

# Enable optimizations
target_compile_options(cahn_hilliard PRIVATE -O3 -march=native -Wall -Wextra)

# Link math library
target_link_libraries(cahn_hilliard m)
#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

// 3D index calculation
inline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {
    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;
}

// Compute Laplacian with clamped boundary conditions
double computeLaplacian(const std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
                        const double dx, const double dy, const double dz, const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z) {
    const size_t xp = (x < nx - 1) ? x + 1 : x;
    const size_t yp = (y < ny - 1) ? y + 1 : y;
    const size_t zp = (z < nz - 1) ? z + 1 : z;
    const size_t xn = (x > 0) ? x - 1 : 0;
    const size_t yn = (y > 0) ? y - 1 : 0;
    const size_t zn = (z > 0) ? z - 1 : 0;
    
    const double cxx = (c[idx3(xp, y, z, nx, ny)] + c[idx3(xn, y, z, nx, ny)] - 
                  2.0 * c[idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny)]) / (dx * dx);
    const double cyy = (c[idx3(x, yp, z, nx, ny)] + c[idx3(x, yn, z, nx, ny)] - 
                  2.0 * c[idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny)]) / (dy * dy);
    const double czz = (c[idx3(x, y, zp, nx, ny)] + c[idx3(x, y, zn, nx, ny)] - 
                  2.0 * c[idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny)]) / (dz * dz);
    
    return cxx + cyy + czz;
}

// Compute chemical potential
void computeChemicalPotential(const std::vector<double>& c, std::vector<double>& mu,
                              const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
                              const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,
                              const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {
    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
                const double cv = c[idx];
                
                mu[idx] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB) 
                         + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv
                         - gamma * computeLaplacian(c, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, z);
            }
        }
    }
}

// Cahn-Hilliard update step
void cahnHilliardUpdate(std::vector<double>& cnew, const std::vector<double>& cold,
                        const std::vector<double>& mu,
                        const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
                        const double D, const double dt, const double dx, const double dy, const double dz) {
    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
                cnew[idx] = cold[idx] + dt * D * 
                           computeLaplacian(mu, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, z);
            }
        }
    }
}

// Initialize concentration field
void initializeConcentration(std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {
    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;
    
    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
                // Generate pseudo-random value in [-1, 1]
                const size_t linear_id = z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;
                const double pseudo = ((((linear_id + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));
                c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;
            }
        }
    }
}

bool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {
    // Check for NaN or Inf
    for (const auto& val : c) {
        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {
            printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\n");
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    // Check if values are in reasonable range for concentration field
    // After Cahn-Hilliard evolution, values should typically remain bounded
    double minVal = c[0];
    double maxVal = c[0];
    for (const auto& val : c) {
        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);
        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);
    }
    
    printf("Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\n", minVal, maxVal);
    
    // Values should generally stay within reasonable bounds
    if (maxVal > 10.0 || minVal < -10.0) {
        printf("Validation failed: values out of expected range\n");
        return false;
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 64)\n");
    printf("  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -i <num>     Number of time steps (default: 20)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t nx = 64;
    size_t ny = 0;
    size_t nz = 0;
    int iterations = 20;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-x") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-y") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-z") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-i") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;
    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;
    
    printf("Cahn-Hilliard Phase Separation Benchmark\n");
    printf("Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\n", nx, ny, nz);
    printf("Time steps: %d\n", iterations);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Physical parameters
    const double dx = 1.0;
    const double dy = 1.0;
    const double dz = 1.0;
    const double dt = 0.01;
    const double e_AA = -(2.0 / 9.0);
    const double e_BB = -(2.0 / 9.0);
    const double e_AB = (2.0 / 9.0);
    const double gamma = 0.5;
    const double D = 1.0;
    
    size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;
    
    // Allocate arrays
    std::vector<double> cold(gridSize);
    std::vector<double> cnew(gridSize);
    std::vector<double> mu(gridSize);
    
    // Initialize concentration field
    printf("Initializing concentration field...\n");
    initializeConcentration(cold, nx, ny, nz);
    
    // Run simulation
    printf("Running Cahn-Hilliard simulation...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    for (int t = 0; t < iterations; ++t) {
        // Compute chemical potential
        computeChemicalPotential(cold, mu, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, 
                                gamma, e_AA, e_BB, e_AB);
        
        // Update concentration
        cahnHilliardUpdate(cnew, cold, mu, nx, ny, nz, D, dt, dx, dy, dz);
        
        // Swap buffers
        std::swap(cold, cnew);
    }
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate performance
    double cellUpdates = (double)gridSize * iterations;
    double mcups = cellUpdates / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e6;
    printf("Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\n", mcups);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(cold, "Concentration");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(cold, nx, ny, nz);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

/bin/bash -lc 'cat cahn-hilliard/cahn_hilliard.cpp' in /home/llmtest/evals/20261006-204623/cahn-hilliard_gpt-6.1-sol-xhigh_omp_r1
 succeeded in 0ms:
#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

// 3D index calculation
inline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {
    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;
}

// Compute Laplacian with clamped boundary conditions
double computeLaplacian(const std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
                        const double dx, const double dy, const double dz, const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z) {
    const size_t xp = (x < nx - 1) ? x + 1 : x;
    const size_t yp = (y < ny - 1) ? y + 1 : y;
    const size_t zp = (z < nz - 1) ? z + 1 : z;
    const size_t xn = (x > 0) ? x - 1 : 0;
    const size_t yn = (y > 0) ? y - 1 : 0;
    const size_t zn = (z > 0) ? z - 1 : 0;
    
    const double cxx = (c[idx3(xp, y, z, nx, ny)] + c[idx3(xn, y, z, nx, ny)] - 
                  2.0 * c[idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny)]) / (dx * dx);
    const double cyy = (c[idx3(x, yp, z, nx, ny)] + c[idx3(x, yn, z, nx, ny)] - 
                  2.0 * c[idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny)]) / (dy * dy);
    const double czz = (c[idx3(x, y, zp, nx, ny)] + c[idx3(x, y, zn, nx, ny)] - 
                  2.0 * c[idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny)]) / (dz * dz);
    
    return cxx + cyy + czz;
}

// Compute chemical potential
void computeChemicalPotential(const std::vector<double>& c, std::vector<double>& mu,
                              const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
                              const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,
                              const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {
    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
                const double cv = c[idx];
                
                mu[idx] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB) 
                         + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv
                         - gamma * computeLaplacian(c, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, z);
            }
        }
    }
}

// Cahn-Hilliard update step
void cahnHilliardUpdate(std::vector<double>& cnew, const std::vector<double>& cold,
                        const std::vector<double>& mu,
                        const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
                        const double D, const double dt, const double dx, const double dy, const double dz) {
    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
                cnew[idx] = cold[idx] + dt * D * 
                           computeLaplacian(mu, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, z);
            }
        }
    }
}

// Initialize concentration field
void initializeConcentration(std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {
    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;
    
    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
                // Generate pseudo-random value in [-1, 1]
                const size_t linear_id = z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;
                const double pseudo = ((((linear_id + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));
                c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;
            }
        }
    }
}

bool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {
    // Check for NaN or Inf
    for (const auto& val : c) {
        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {
            printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\n");
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    // Check if values are in reasonable range for concentration field
    // After Cahn-Hilliard evolution, values should typically remain bounded
    double minVal = c[0];
    double maxVal = c[0];
    for (const auto& val : c) {
        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);
        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);
    }
    
    printf("Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\n", minVal, maxVal);
    
    // Values should generally stay within reasonable bounds
    if (maxVal > 10.0 || minVal < -10.0) {
        printf("Validation failed: values out of expected range\n");
        return false;
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 64)\n");
    printf("  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -i <num>     Number of time steps (default: 20)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t nx = 64;
    size_t ny = 0;
    size_t nz = 0;
    int iterations = 20;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-x") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-y") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-z") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-i") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;
    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;
    
    printf("Cahn-Hilliard Phase Separation Benchmark\n");
    printf("Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\n", nx, ny, nz);
    printf("Time steps: %d\n", iterations);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Physical parameters
    const double dx = 1.0;
    const double dy = 1.0;
    const double dz = 1.0;
    const double dt = 0.01;
    const double e_AA = -(2.0 / 9.0);
    const double e_BB = -(2.0 / 9.0);
    const double e_AB = (2.0 / 9.0);
    const double gamma = 0.5;
    const double D = 1.0;
    
    size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;
    
    // Allocate arrays
    std::vector<double> cold(gridSize);
    std::vector<double> cnew(gridSize);
    std::vector<double> mu(gridSize);
    
    // Initialize concentration field
    printf("Initializing concentration field...\n");
    initializeConcentration(cold, nx, ny, nz);
    
    // Run simulation
    printf("Running Cahn-Hilliard simulation...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    for (int t = 0; t < iterations; ++t) {
        // Compute chemical potential
        computeChemicalPotential(cold, mu, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, 
                                gamma, e_AA, e_BB, e_AB);
        
        // Update concentration
        cahnHilliardUpdate(cnew, cold, mu, nx, ny, nz, D, dt, dx, dy, dz);
        
        // Swap buffers
        std::swap(cold, cnew);
    }
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate performance
    double cellUpdates = (double)gridSize * iterations;
    double mcups = cellUpdates / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e6;
    printf("Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\n", mcups);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(cold, "Concentration");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(cold, nx, ny, nz);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

+// All threads in the simulation team call this worksharing kernel. The implicit
+// barrier completes the entire stencil pass before its output is read.
+template <typename Store>
+void applyLaplacian(const double* field, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
+                    const double dx, const double dy, const double dz, Store store) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const double dx2 = dx * dx;
+    const double dy2 = dy * dy;
+    const double dz2 = dz * dz;
+
+    #pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
+    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {

+            if (nx == 0) continue;
+            const size_t base = z * plane + y * nx;
+            const double* row = field + base;
+            const double* yn = (y > 0) ? row - nx : row;
+            const double* yp = (y + 1 < ny) ? row + nx : row;
+            const double* zn = (z > 0) ? row - plane : row;
+            const double* zp = (z + 1 < nz) ? row + plane : row;
+
+            store(base, rowLaplacian(row, yn, yp, zn, zp, 0, 0, nx > 1 ? 1 : 0, dx2, dy2, dz2));

+            for (size_t x = 1; x < nx - 1; ++x) {

+

+// Row pointers keep boundary/index calculations out of the SIMD loop.
+inline double rowLaplacian(const double* row, const double* yn, const double* yp,
+                          const double* zn, const double* zp,
+                          const size_t x, const size_t xn, const size_t xp,

+    const double czz = (zp[x] + zn[x] - 2.0 * row[x]) / dz2;

+// All threads in the simulation team call this worksharing kernel. The implicit
+// barrier completes the entire stencil pass before its output is read.
+template <typename Store>
+void applyLaplacian(const double* field, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
+                    const double dx, const double dy, const double dz, Store store) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const double dx2 = dx * dx;
+    const double dy2 = dy * dy;
+    const double dz2 = dz * dz;
+
+    #pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
+    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
+        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
+            if (nx == 0) continue;
+            const size_t base = z * plane + y * nx;
+            const double* row = field + base;
+            const double* yn = (y > 0) ? row - nx : row;
+            const double* yp = (y + 1 < ny) ? row + nx : row;
+            const double* zn = (z > 0) ? row - plane : row;
+            const double* zp = (z + 1 < nz) ? row + plane : row;
+
+            store(base, rowLaplacian(row, yn, yp, zn, zp, 0, 0, nx > 1 ? 1 : 0, dx2, dy2, dz2));
+
+            #pragma omp simd
+            for (size_t x = 1; x < nx - 1; ++x) {
+                store(base + x, rowLaplacian(row, yn, yp, zn, zp, x, x - 1, x + 1, dx2, dy2, dz2));
+            }
+
+            if (nx > 1) {
+                const size_t x = nx - 1;
+                store(base + x, rowLaplacian(row, yn, yp, zn, zp, x, x - 1, x, dx2, dy2, dz2));
+            }
+        }
+    }
 }

+template <typename Store>
+void applyLaplacian(const double* field, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
+                    const double dx, const double dy, const double dz, Store store) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const double dx2 = dx * dx;
+    const double dy2 = dy * dy;
+    const double dz2 = dz * dz;
+
+    #pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
+    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
+        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
+            if (nx == 0) continue;
+            const size_t base = z * plane + y * nx;
+            const double* row = field + base;
+            const double* yn = (y > 0) ? row - nx : row;
+            const double* yp = (y + 1 < ny) ? row + nx : row;
+            const double* zn = (z > 0) ? row - plane : row;
+            const double* zp = (z + 1 < nz) ? row + plane : row;
+
+            store(base, rowLaplacian(row, yn, yp, zn, zp, 0, 0, nx > 1 ? 1 : 0, dx2, dy2, dz2));
+
+            #pragma omp simd
+            for (size_t x = 1; x < nx - 1; ++x) {
+                store(base + x, rowLaplacian(row, yn, yp, zn, zp, x, x - 1, x + 1, dx2, dy2, dz2));
+            }
+
+            if (nx > 1) {
+                const size_t x = nx - 1;
+                store(base + x, rowLaplacian(row, yn, yp, zn, zp, x, x - 1, x, dx2, dy2, dz2));
+            }
+        }
+    }

#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

// Row pointers keep boundary/index calculations out of the SIMD loop.
inline double rowLaplacian(const double* row, const double* yn, const double* yp,
                          const double* zn, const double* zp,
                          const size_t x, const size_t xn, const size_t xp,
                          const double dx2, const double dy2, const double dz2) {
    const double cxx = (row[xp] + row[xn] - 2.0 * row[x]) / dx2;
    const double cyy = (yp[x] + yn[x] - 2.0 * row[x]) / dy2;
    const double czz = (zp[x] + zn[x] - 2.0 * row[x]) / dz2;
    return cxx + cyy + czz;
}

// All threads in the simulation team call this worksharing kernel. The implicit
// barrier completes the entire stencil pass before its output is read.
template <typename Store>
void applyLaplacian(const double* field, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
                    const double dx, const double dy, const double dz, Store store) {
    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
    const double dx2 = dx * dx;
    const double dy2 = dy * dy;
    const double dz2 = dz * dz;

    #pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
            if (nx == 0) continue;
            const size_t base = z * plane + y * nx;
            const double* row = field + base;
            const double* yn = (y > 0) ? row - nx : row;
            const double* yp = (y + 1 < ny) ? row + nx : row;
            const double* zn = (z > 0) ? row - plane : row;
            const double* zp = (z + 1 < nz) ? row + plane : row;

            store(base, rowLaplacian(row, yn, yp, zn, zp, 0, 0, nx > 1 ? 1 : 0, dx2, dy2, dz2));

            #pragma omp simd
            for (size_t x = 1; x < nx - 1; ++x) {
                store(base + x, rowLaplacian(row, yn, yp, zn, zp, x, x - 1, x + 1, dx2, dy2, dz2));
            }

            if (nx > 1) {
                const size_t x = nx - 1;
                store(base + x, rowLaplacian(row, yn, yp, zn, zp, x, x - 1, x, dx2, dy2, dz2));
            }
        }
    }
}

// Compute chemical potential within the persistent OpenMP team.
void computeChemicalPotential(const std::vector<double>& c, std::vector<double>& mu,
                              const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
                              const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,
                              const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {
    const double* concentration = c.data();
    double* potential = mu.data();
    applyLaplacian(concentration, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, [=](size_t idx, double laplacian) {
        const double cv = concentration[idx];
        potential[idx] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB)
                         + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv - gamma * laplacian;
    });
}

// Cahn-Hilliard update step
void cahnHilliardUpdate(std::vector<double>& cnew, const std::vector<double>& cold,
                        const std::vector<double>& mu,
                        const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
                        const double D, const double dt, const double dx, const double dy, const double dz) {
    const double* previous = cold.data();
    double* next = cnew.data();
    applyLaplacian(mu.data(), nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, [=](size_t idx, double laplacian) {
        next[idx] = previous[idx] + dt * D * laplacian;
    });
}

// Initialize concentration field
void initializeConcentration(std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {
    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;
    
    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
    for (size_t idx = 0; idx < vol; ++idx) {
        // Preserve the original deterministic pseudo-random sequence.
        const double pseudo = ((((idx + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));
        c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;
    }
}

bool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {
    // Check for NaN or Inf
    for (const auto& val : c) {
        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {
            printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\n");
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    // Check if values are in reasonable range for concentration field
    // After Cahn-Hilliard evolution, values should typically remain bounded
    double minVal = c[0];
    double maxVal = c[0];
    for (const auto& val : c) {
        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);
        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);
    }
    
    printf("Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\n", minVal, maxVal);
    
    // Values should generally stay within reasonable bounds
    if (maxVal > 10.0 || minVal < -10.0) {
        printf("Validation failed: values out of expected range\n");
        return false;
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 64)\n");
    printf("  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -i <num>     Number of time steps (default: 20)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t nx = 64;
    size_t ny = 0;
    size_t nz = 0;
    int iterations = 20;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-x") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-y") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-z") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-i") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;
    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;
    
    printf("Cahn-Hilliard Phase Separation Benchmark\n");
    printf("Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\n", nx, ny, nz);
    printf("Time steps: %d\n", iterations);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Physical parameters
    const double dx = 1.0;
    const double dy = 1.0;
    const double dz = 1.0;
    const double dt = 0.01;
    const double e_AA = -(2.0 / 9.0);
    const double e_BB = -(2.0 / 9.0);
    const double e_AB = (2.0 / 9.0);
    const double gamma = 0.5;
    const double D = 1.0;
    
    size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;
    
    // Allocate arrays
    std::vector<double> cold(gridSize);
    std::vector<double> cnew(gridSize);
    std::vector<double> mu(gridSize);
    
    // Initialize concentration field
    printf("Initializing concentration field...\n");
    initializeConcentration(cold, nx, ny, nz);
    
    // Run simulation
    printf("Running Cahn-Hilliard simulation...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    #pragma omp parallel
    {
        // Private buffer pointers avoid a shared swap and an extra barrier.
        auto* current = &cold;
        auto* next = &cnew;
        for (int t = 0; t < iterations; ++t) {
            computeChemicalPotential(*current, mu, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz,
                                     gamma, e_AA, e_BB, e_AB);
            cahnHilliardUpdate(*next, *current, mu, nx, ny, nz, D, dt, dx, dy, dz);
            std::swap(current, next);
        }
    }
    if (iterations > 0 && iterations % 2 != 0) std::swap(cold, cnew);
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate performance
    double cellUpdates = (double)gridSize * iterations;
    double mcups = cellUpdates / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e6;
    printf("Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\n", mcups);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(cold, "Concentration");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(cold, nx, ny, nz);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

+// All threads in the simulation team call this worksharing kernel. The implicit
+// barrier completes the entire stencil pass before its output is read.
+template <typename Store>
+void applyLaplacian(const double* field, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
+                    const double dx, const double dy, const double dz, Store store) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const double dx2 = dx * dx;
+    const double dy2 = dy * dy;
+    const double dz2 = dz * dz;
+
+    #pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
+    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
+        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
+            if (nx == 0) continue;
+            const size_t base = z * plane + y * nx;
+            const double* row = field + base;
+            const double* yn = (y > 0) ? row - nx : row;
+            const double* yp = (y + 1 < ny) ? row + nx : row;
+            const double* zn = (z > 0) ? row - plane : row;
+            const double* zp = (z + 1 < nz) ? row + plane : row;
+
+            store(base, rowLaplacian(row, yn, yp, zn, zp, 0, 0, nx > 1 ? 1 : 0, dx2, dy2, dz2));
+
+            #pragma omp simd
+            for (size_t x = 1; x < nx - 1; ++x) {
+                store(base + x, rowLaplacian(row, yn, yp, zn, zp, x, x - 1, x + 1, dx2, dy2, dz2));
+            }
+
+            if (nx > 1) {
+                const size_t x = nx - 1;
+                store(base + x, rowLaplacian(row, yn, yp, zn, zp, x, x - 1, x, dx2, dy2, dz2));
+            }
+        }
+    }
 }

+// All threads in the simulation team call this worksharing kernel. The implicit
+// barrier completes the entire stencil pass before its output is read.
+template <typename Store>
+void applyLaplacian(const double* field, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
+                    const double dx, const double dy, const double dz, Store store) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const double dx2 = dx * dx;
+    const double dy2 = dy * dy;
+    const double dz2 = dz * dz;
+
+    #pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
+    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
+        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
+            if (nx == 0) continue;
+            const size_t base = z * plane + y * nx;
+            const double* row = field + base;
+            const double* yn = (y > 0) ? row - nx : row;
+            const double* yp = (y + 1 < ny) ? row + nx : row;
+            const double* zn = (z > 0) ? row - plane : row;
+            const double* zp = (z + 1 < nz) ? row + plane : row;
+
+            store(base, rowLaplacian(row, yn, yp, zn, zp, 0, 0, nx > 1 ? 1 : 0, dx2, dy2, dz2));
+
+            #pragma omp simd
+            for (size_t x = 1; x < nx - 1; ++x) {
+                store(base + x, rowLaplacian(row, yn, yp, zn, zp, x, x - 1, x + 1, dx2, dy2, dz2));
+            }
+
+            if (nx > 1) {
+                const size_t x = nx - 1;
+                store(base + x, rowLaplacian(row, yn, yp, zn, zp, x, x - 1, x, dx2, dy2, dz2));
+            }
+        }
+    }
 }

+// Compute chemical potential within the persistent OpenMP team.
+[[gnu::always_inline]] inline void computeChemicalPotential(const std::vector<double>& c, std::vector<double>& mu,
+                              const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
+                              const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,
+                              const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {

codex
The row kernels are vectorized, and the first version reduced a 64³, 200-step run from 342 ms to 9 ms on 32 cores. I also found unnecessary runtime divisions for the fixed unit grid spacing; forced inlining removes them. I’ll recheck correctness and performance after that change.

+// All threads in the simulation team call this worksharing kernel. The implicit
+// barrier completes the entire stencil pass before its output is read.
+// Inline the kernels so the fixed physical parameters (including unit grid

+[[gnu::always_inline]] inline void applyLaplacian(const double* field, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,

+    const double dy2 = dy * dy;

+// Compute chemical potential within the persistent OpenMP team.
+[[gnu::always_inline]] inline void computeChemicalPotential(const std::vector<double>& c, std::vector<double>& mu,
+                              const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
+                              const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,
+                              const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {
+    const double* concentration = c.data();
+    double* potential = mu.data();
+    applyLaplacian(concentration, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, [=](size_t idx, double laplacian) {
+        const double cv = concentration[idx];
+        potential[idx] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB)
+                         + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv - gamma * laplacian;
+    });
+}

+// All threads in the simulation team call this worksharing kernel. The implicit

+// Compute chemical potential within the persistent OpenMP team.
+[[gnu::always_inline]] inline void computeChemicalPotential(const std::vector<double>& c, std::vector<double>& mu,
+                              const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
+                              const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,
+                              const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {
+    const double* concentration = c.data();
+    double* potential = mu.data();
+    applyLaplacian(concentration, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, [=](size_t idx, double laplacian) {
+        const double cv = concentration[idx];
+        potential[idx] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB)
+                         + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv - gamma * laplacian;
+    });

+// Compute chemical potential within the persistent OpenMP team.
+[[gnu::always_inline]] inline void computeChemicalPotential(const double* c, double* mu,
+                              const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
+                              const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,
+                              const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {

+// All threads in the simulation team call this worksharing kernel. The implicit
+// barrier completes the entire stencil pass before its output is read.
+// Inline the kernels so the fixed physical parameters (including unit grid
+// spacing) are folded into the vectorized loops rather than divided at runtime.
+template <typename Store>
+[[gnu::always_inline]] inline void applyLaplacian(const double* field, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
+                    const double dx, const double dy, const double dz, Store store) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const double dx2 = dx * dx;
+    const double dy2 = dy * dy;
+    const double dz2 = dz * dz;
+
+    #pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
     for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
         for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
-            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
-                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
-                const double cv = c[idx];
-                
-                mu[idx] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB) 
-                         + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv
-                         - gamma * computeLaplacian(c, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, z);
+            if (nx == 0) continue;
+            const size_t base = z * plane + y * nx;
+            const double* row = field + base;
+            const double* yn = (y > 0) ? row - nx : row;
+            const double* yp = (y + 1 < ny) ? row + nx : row;
+            const double* zn = (z > 0) ? row - plane : row;
+            const double* zp = (z + 1 < nz) ? row + plane : row;
+
+            store(base, rowLaplacian(row, yn, yp, zn, zp, 0, 0, nx > 1 ? 1 : 0, dx2, dy2, dz2));
+
+            #pragma omp simd
+            for (size_t x = 1; x < nx - 1; ++x) {
+                store(base + x, rowLaplacian(row, yn, yp, zn, zp, x, x - 1, x + 1, dx2, dy2, dz2));
+            }
+
+            if (nx > 1) {
+                const size_t x = nx - 1;
+                store(base + x, rowLaplacian(row, yn, yp, zn, zp, x, x - 1, x, dx2, dy2, dz2));
             }
         }
     }
 }
 
+// Compute chemical potential within the persistent OpenMP team.
+[[gnu::always_inline]] inline void computeChemicalPotential(const double* c, double* mu,
+                              const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
+                              const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,
+                              const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {
+    applyLaplacian(c, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, [=](size_t idx, double laplacian) {
+        const double cv = c[idx];
+        mu[idx] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB)
+                         + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv - gamma * laplacian;
+    });

+// Row pointers keep boundary/index calculations out of the SIMD loop.
+inline double rowLaplacian(const double* row, const double* yn, const double* yp,
+                          const double* zn, const double* zp,
+                          const size_t x, const size_t xn, const size_t xp,
+                          const double dx2, const double dy2, const double dz2) {
+    const double cxx = (row[xp] + row[xn] - 2.0 * row[x]) / dx2;
+    const double cyy = (yp[x] + yn[x] - 2.0 * row[x]) / dy2;

-// Compute chemical potential
-void computeChemicalPotential(const std::vector<double>& c, std::vector<double>& mu,

-                              const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,
-                              const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {
+// All threads in the simulation team call this worksharing kernel. The implicit
+// barrier completes the entire stencil pass before its output is read.
+// Inline the kernels so the fixed physical parameters (including unit grid
+// spacing) are folded into the vectorized loops rather than divided at runtime.
+template <typename Store>
+[[gnu::always_inline]] inline void applyLaplacian(const double* field, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
+                    const double dx, const double dy, const double dz, Store store) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const double dx2 = dx * dx;
+    const double dy2 = dy * dy;
+    const double dz2 = dz * dz;
+
+    #pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
     for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
         for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
-            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
-                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
-                const double cv = c[idx];
-                
-                mu[idx] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB) 
-                         + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv
-                         - gamma * computeLaplacian(c, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, z);
+            if (nx == 0) continue;
+            const size_t base = z * plane + y * nx;
+            const double* row = field + base;
+            const double* yn = (y > 0) ? row - nx : row;
+            const double* yp = (y + 1 < ny) ? row + nx : row;
+            const double* zn = (z > 0) ? row - plane : row;
+            const double* zp = (z + 1 < nz) ? row + plane : row;
+
+            store(base, rowLaplacian(row, yn, yp, zn, zp, 0, 0, nx > 1 ? 1 : 0, dx2, dy2, dz2));
+
+            #pragma omp simd
+            for (size_t x = 1; x < nx - 1; ++x) {
+                store(base + x, rowLaplacian(row, yn, yp, zn, zp, x, x - 1, x + 1, dx2, dy2, dz2));
+            }
+
+            if (nx > 1) {
+                const size_t x = nx - 1;
+                store(base + x, rowLaplacian(row, yn, yp, zn, zp, x, x - 1, x, dx2, dy2, dz2));
             }
         }
     }
 }

+// All threads in the simulation team call this worksharing kernel. The implicit
+// barrier completes the entire stencil pass before its output is read.
+// Inline the kernels so the fixed physical parameters (including unit grid
+// spacing) are folded into the vectorized loops rather than divided at runtime.
+template <typename Store>
+[[gnu::always_inline]] inline void applyLaplacian(const double* field, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
+                    const double dx, const double dy, const double dz, Store store) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const double dx2 = dx * dx;
+    const double dy2 = dy * dy;
+    const double dz2 = dz * dz;
+
+    #pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)

-                const double cv = c[idx];
-

-                         + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv
-                         - gamma * computeLaplacian(c, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, z);
+            if (nx == 0) continue;
+            const size_t base = z * plane + y * nx;
+            const double* row = field + base;
+            const double* yn = (y > 0) ? row - nx : row;
+            const double* yp = (y + 1 < ny) ? row + nx : row;
+            const double* zn = (z > 0) ? row - plane : row;
+            const double* zp = (z + 1 < nz) ? row + plane : row;
+
+            store(base, rowLaplacian(row, yn, yp, zn, zp, 0, 0, nx > 1 ? 1 : 0, dx2, dy2, dz2));
+
+            #pragma omp simd
+            for (size_t x = 1; x < nx - 1; ++x) {
+                store(base + x, rowLaplacian(row, yn, yp, zn, zp, x, x - 1, x + 1, dx2, dy2, dz2));
+            }
+
+            if (nx > 1) {
+                const size_t x = nx - 1;
+                store(base + x, rowLaplacian(row, yn, yp, zn, zp, x, x - 1, x, dx2, dy2, dz2));
             }
         }
     }
 }
 
+// Compute chemical potential within the persistent OpenMP team.
+[[gnu::always_inline]] inline void computeChemicalPotential(const double* c, double* mu,
+                              const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
+                              const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,
+                              const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {
+    applyLaplacian(c, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, [=](size_t idx, double laplacian) {
+        const double cv = c[idx];
+        mu[idx] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB)
+                         + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv - gamma * laplacian;
+    });

    99	bool validateResult(const std::span<const double> c, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {
   100	    // Check for NaN or Inf
   101	    for (const auto& val : c) {
   102	        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {
   103	            printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\n");
   104	            return false;
   105	        }
   106	    }
   107	    
   108	    // Check if values are in reasonable range for concentration field
   109	    // After Cahn-Hilliard evolution, values should typically remain bounded
   110	    double minVal = c[0];
   111	    double maxVal = c[0];
   112	    for (const auto& val : c) {
   113	        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);
   114	        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);
   115	    }
   116	    
   117	    printf("Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\n", minVal, maxVal);
   118	    
   119	    // Values should generally stay within reasonable bounds
   120	    if (maxVal > 10.0 || minVal < -10.0) {
   121	        printf("Validation failed: values out of expected range\n");
   122	        return false;
   123	    }
   124	    
   125	    return true;
   126	}

   192	    
   193	    // Allocate without serial zero-fills. Parallel initialization places pages
   194	    // on the workers' NUMA nodes and keeps page faults outside the timed loop.
   195	    auto cold = std::make_unique_for_overwrite<double[]>(gridSize);
   196	    auto cnew = std::make_unique_for_overwrite<double[]>(gridSize);
   197	    auto mu = std::make_unique_for_overwrite<double[]>(gridSize);
   198	    
   199	    // Initialize concentration field
   200	    printf("Initializing concentration field...\n");
   201	    initializeConcentration(cold.get(), cnew.get(), mu.get(), nx, ny, nz);
   202	    
   203	    // Run simulation
   204	    printf("Running Cahn-Hilliard simulation...\n");
   205	    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
   206	    
   207	    #pragma omp parallel
   208	    {
   209	        // Private buffer pointers avoid a shared swap and an extra barrier.
   210	        double* current = cold.get();
   211	        double* next = cnew.get();
   212	        for (int t = 0; t < iterations; ++t) {
   213	            computeChemicalPotential(current, mu.get(), nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz,
   214	                                     gamma, e_AA, e_BB, e_AB);
   215	            cahnHilliardUpdate(next, current, mu.get(), nx, ny, nz, D, dt, dx, dy, dz);
   216	            std::swap(current, next);
   217	        }
   218	    }
   219	    if (iterations > 0 && iterations % 2 != 0) std::swap(cold, cnew);
   220	    
   221	    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
   222	    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
   223	    
   224	    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
   225	    
   226	    // Calculate performance
   227	    double cellUpdates = (double)gridSize * iterations;
   228	    double mcups = cellUpdates / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e6;
   229	    printf("Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\n", mcups);
   230	    
   231	    // Print results for external validation
   232	    if (printResults) {
   233	        // Reuse the existing reporting interface outside the timed simulation.
   234	        // Release scratch buffers first to keep peak memory within three grids.
   235	        cnew.reset();
   236	        mu.reset();
   237	        const std::vector<double> result(cold.get(), cold.get() + gridSize);
   238	        print_results(result, "Concentration");
   239	    }
   240	    
   241	    // Validation
   242	    if (validate) {
   243	        printf("Validating result...\n");
   244	        bool valid = validateResult(std::span<const double>(cold.get(), gridSize), nx, ny, nz);
   245	        
   246	        if (valid) {
   247	            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
   248	            return 0;
   249	        } else {
   250	            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
   251	            return 1;
   252	        }
   253	    }
   254	    
   255	    return 0;
   256	}

-// Compute chemical potential
-void computeChemicalPotential(const std::vector<double>& c, std::vector<double>& mu,

+// All threads in the simulation team call this worksharing kernel. The implicit
+// barrier completes the entire stencil pass before its output is read.
+// Inline the kernels so the fixed physical parameters (including unit grid
+// spacing) are folded into the vectorized loops rather than divided at runtime.
+template <typename Store>
+[[gnu::always_inline]] inline void applyLaplacian(const double* field, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
+                    const double dx, const double dy, const double dz, Store store) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const double dx2 = dx * dx;
+    const double dy2 = dy * dy;
+    const double dz2 = dz * dz;
+
+    #pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)

         for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
-            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
-                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
-                const double cv = c[idx];
-                
-                mu[idx] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB) 
-                         + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv
-                         - gamma * computeLaplacian(c, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, z);
+            if (nx == 0) continue;
+            const size_t base = z * plane + y * nx;
+            const double* row = field + base;
+            const double* yn = (y > 0) ? row - nx : row;
+            const double* yp = (y + 1 < ny) ? row + nx : row;
+            const double* zn = (z > 0) ? row - plane : row;
+            const double* zp = (z + 1 < nz) ? row + plane : row;
+
+            store(base, rowLaplacian(row, yn, yp, zn, zp, 0, 0, nx > 1 ? 1 : 0, dx2, dy2, dz2));
+
+            #pragma omp simd
+            for (size_t x = 1; x < nx - 1; ++x) {
+                store(base + x, rowLaplacian(row, yn, yp, zn, zp, x, x - 1, x + 1, dx2, dy2, dz2));
+            }
+
+            if (nx > 1) {
+                const size_t x = nx - 1;
+                store(base + x, rowLaplacian(row, yn, yp, zn, zp, x, x - 1, x, dx2, dy2, dz2));
             }
         }
     }
 }
 
+// Compute chemical potential within the persistent OpenMP team.
+[[gnu::always_inline]] inline void computeChemicalPotential(const double* c, double* mu,
+                              const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
+                              const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,
+                              const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {
+    applyLaplacian(c, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, [=](size_t idx, double laplacian) {
+        const double cv = c[idx];
+        mu[idx] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB)
+                         + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv - gamma * laplacian;
+    });

+            store(base, rowLaplacian(row, yn, yp, zn, zp, 0, 0, nx > 1 ? 1 : 0, dx2, dy2, dz2));
+
+            #pragma omp simd
+            for (size_t x = 1; x < nx - 1; ++x) {
+                store(base + x, rowLaplacian(row, yn, yp, zn, zp, x, x - 1, x + 1, dx2, dy2, dz2));
+            }
+
+            if (nx > 1) {
+                const size_t x = nx - 1;
+                store(base + x, rowLaplacian(row, yn, yp, zn, zp, x, x - 1, x, dx2, dy2, dz2));
             }
         }
     }
 }

+[[gnu::always_inline]] inline void computeChemicalPotential(const double* c, double* mu,

+                              const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,
+                              const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {
+    applyLaplacian(c, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, [=](size_t idx, double laplacian) {

The final smoke test found that OpenMP’s default of 256 logical CPUs slowed the small default grid. I’ll size the default team to the workload while continuing to honor explicit `OMP_NUM_THREADS` settings.

+// Compute chemical potential within the persistent OpenMP team.
+[[gnu::always_inline]] inline void computeChemicalPotential(const double* c, double* mu,
+                              const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
+                              const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,
+                              const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {
+    applyLaplacian(c, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, [=](size_t idx, double laplacian) {
+        const double cv = c[idx];
+        mu[idx] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB)
+                         + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv - gamma * laplacian;
+    });

-// Compute chemical potential
-void computeChemicalPotential(const std::vector<double>& c, std::vector<double>& mu,

+// All threads in the simulation team call this worksharing kernel. The implicit
+// barrier completes the entire stencil pass before its output is read.
+// Inline the kernels so the fixed physical parameters (including unit grid
+// spacing) are folded into the vectorized loops rather than divided at runtime.
+template <typename Store>
+[[gnu::always_inline]] inline void applyLaplacian(
+    const double* field, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
+    const double dx, const double dy, const double dz, Store store) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const double dx2 = dx * dx;
+    const double dy2 = dy * dy;
+    const double dz2 = dz * dz;
+
+    #pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
     for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
         for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
-            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
-                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
-                const double cv = c[idx];
-                
-                mu[idx] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB) 
-                         + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv
-                         - gamma * computeLaplacian(c, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, z);
+            if (nx == 0) continue;
+            const size_t base = z * plane + y * nx;
+            const double* row = field + base;
+            const double* yn = (y > 0) ? row - nx : row;
+            const double* yp = (y + 1 < ny) ? row + nx : row;
+            const double* zn = (z > 0) ? row - plane : row;
+            const double* zp = (z + 1 < nz) ? row + plane : row;
+
+            store(base, rowLaplacian(row, yn, yp, zn, zp, 0, 0, nx > 1 ? 1 : 0, dx2, dy2, dz2));
+
+            #pragma omp simd
+            for (size_t x = 1; x < nx - 1; ++x) {
+                store(base + x, rowLaplacian(row, yn, yp, zn, zp, x, x - 1, x + 1, dx2, dy2, dz2));
+            }
+
+            if (nx > 1) {
+                const size_t x = nx - 1;
+                store(base + x, rowLaplacian(row, yn, yp, zn, zp, x, x - 1, x, dx2, dy2, dz2));
             }
         }
     }
 }
 
+// Compute chemical potential within the persistent OpenMP team.
+[[gnu::always_inline]] inline void computeChemicalPotential(
+    const double* c, double* mu, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
+    const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,
+    const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {
+    applyLaplacian(c, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, [=](size_t idx, double laplacian) {
+        const double cv = c[idx];
+        mu[idx] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB)
+                  + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv - gamma * laplacian;
+    });
+}

+[[gnu::always_inline]] inline void cahnHilliardUpdate(
+    double* cnew, const double* cold, const double* mu,
+    const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
+    const double D, const double dt, const double dx, const double dy, const double dz) {
+    applyLaplacian(mu, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, [=](size_t idx, double laplacian) {
+        cnew[idx] = cold[idx] + dt * D * laplacian;
+    });
 }

original='/tmp/cahn-hilliard-check-pa_vog8l/original'

    expected=subprocess.run([original]+args,capture_output=True,text=True,check=True).stdout
    reference=expected[expected.index('=== RESULTS ==='):]
    for threads in [None,1,2,4,8,16,32]:
        env=base_env if threads is None else dict(base_env,OMP_NUM_THREADS=str(threads),OMP_DYNAMIC='FALSE',OMP_PROC_BIND='close',OMP_PLACES='cores')
        run=subprocess.run([parallel]+args,capture_output=True,text=True,check=True,env=env,timeout=20)
        observed=run.stdout[run.stdout.index('=== RESULTS ==='):]
        assert observed==reference, ((nx,ny,nz,it),threads,reference,observed)
        rate=float(re.search(r'Performance: (\\S+)',run.stdout).group(1))
        assert math.isfinite(rate), ('nonfinite performance',run.stdout)
    print('Exact match:',(nx,ny,nz,it),'with default and 1,2,4,8,16,32 threads',flush=True)
start=time.perf_counter()
run=subprocess.run([parallel,'-v','-r'],env=base_env,capture_output=True,text=True,check=True,timeout=20)
print('Default smoke test wall time:',round(time.perf_counter()-start,3),'s')

+// Row pointers keep boundary/index calculations out of the SIMD loop.
+inline double rowLaplacian(const double* row, const double* yn, const double* yp,
+                          const double* zn, const double* zp,
+                          const size_t x, const size_t xn, const size_t xp,
+                          const double dx2, const double dy2, const double dz2) {
+    const double cxx = (row[xp] + row[xn] - 2.0 * row[x]) / dx2;
+    const double cyy = (yp[x] + yn[x] - 2.0 * row[x]) / dy2;
+    const double czz = (zp[x] + zn[x] - 2.0 * row[x]) / dz2;

-// Compute chemical potential
-void computeChemicalPotential(const std::vector<double>& c, std::vector<double>& mu,
-                              const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
-                              const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,
-                              const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {
+// All threads in the simulation team call this worksharing kernel. The implicit
+// barrier completes the entire stencil pass before its output is read.
+// Inline the kernels so the fixed physical parameters (including unit grid
+// spacing) are folded into the vectorized loops rather than divided at runtime.
+template <typename Store>
+[[gnu::always_inline]] inline void applyLaplacian(
+    const double* field, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
+    const double dx, const double dy, const double dz, Store store) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const double dx2 = dx * dx;
+    const double dy2 = dy * dy;
+    const double dz2 = dz * dz;
+
+    #pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
     for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
         for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
-            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
-                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
-                const double cv = c[idx];
-                
-                mu[idx] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB) 
-                         + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv
-                         - gamma * computeLaplacian(c, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, z);
+            if (nx == 0) continue;
+            const size_t base = z * plane + y * nx;
+            const double* row = field + base;
+            const double* yn = (y > 0) ? row - nx : row;
+            const double* yp = (y + 1 < ny) ? row + nx : row;
+            const double* zn = (z > 0) ? row - plane : row;
+            const double* zp = (z + 1 < nz) ? row + plane : row;
+
+            store(base, rowLaplacian(row, yn, yp, zn, zp, 0, 0, nx > 1 ? 1 : 0, dx2, dy2, dz2));
+
+            #pragma omp simd
+            for (size_t x = 1; x < nx - 1; ++x) {
+                store(base + x, rowLaplacian(row, yn, yp, zn, zp, x, x - 1, x + 1, dx2, dy2, dz2));
+            }
+
+            if (nx > 1) {
+                const size_t x = nx - 1;
+                store(base + x, rowLaplacian(row, yn, yp, zn, zp, x, x - 1, x, dx2, dy2, dz2));
             }
         }
     }
 }

+// All threads in the simulation team call this worksharing kernel. The implicit

+// Inline the kernels so the fixed physical parameters (including unit grid

+template <typename Store>
+[[gnu::always_inline]] inline void applyLaplacian(
+    const double* field, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
+    const double dx, const double dy, const double dz, Store store) {

+    const double dx2 = dx * dx;
+    const double dy2 = dy * dy;
+    const double dz2 = dz * dz;

+            if (nx == 0) continue;
+            const size_t base = z * plane + y * nx;
+            const double* row = field + base;
+            const double* yn = (y > 0) ? row - nx : row;
+            const double* yp = (y + 1 < ny) ? row + nx : row;
+            const double* zn = (z > 0) ? row - plane : row;
+            const double* zp = (z + 1 < nz) ? row + plane : row;
+
+            store(base, rowLaplacian(row, yn, yp, zn, zp, 0, 0, nx > 1 ? 1 : 0, dx2, dy2, dz2));

+                store(base + x, rowLaplacian(row, yn, yp, zn, zp, x, x - 1, x + 1, dx2, dy2, dz2));
+            }
+
+            if (nx > 1) {
+                const size_t x = nx - 1;
+                store(base + x, rowLaplacian(row, yn, yp, zn, zp, x, x - 1, x, dx2, dy2, dz2));
             }

+// Compute chemical potential within the persistent OpenMP team.
+[[gnu::always_inline]] inline void computeChemicalPotential(
+    const double* c, double* mu, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
+    const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,
+    const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {
+    applyLaplacian(c, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, [=](size_t idx, double laplacian) {
+        const double cv = c[idx];
+        mu[idx] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB)
+                  + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv - gamma * laplacian;
+    });

Implemented required OpenMP with persistent threads, SIMD stencil loops, and parallel NUMA initialization. Only the two existing benchmark files changed; executable remains `cahn_hilliard`.

Verified exact serial results across 16 cases with default and 1–32 threads. ASan/UBSan passed.

Measured ~49× speedup here: 64³, 200 steps, 32 threads—342 ms → 7 ms.
